Atomization rules:

1. Every parent FR ID is one row and retains its existing ID.
2. Every numbered source item is one row.
3. Section 19 line 1174 is split into `DATA-64` and `DATA-65` because reconciliation and idempotent retry are independently testable.
4. Section 29 includes four approved OQ rows plus 104 binding decision-register rows. Obsolete recommendation-history blockquotes are excluded.
5. Multi-owner requirements name one primary owner in column 5 and list required secondary contracts through dependency codes.
6. Bucket C N1-N4 remain unimplemented. N3 non-audio residue is not a parent requirement row and receives no completion claim.
